Pseudo-code for _optimize_logits(init, deadline, rng, beta0):
    a = normalize(init)
    z = log(a + 1e-300); z -= mean(z) (to avoid sum scale; softmax(z) = a / sum? If a sum1, exp z / exp z = a yes)
    n=a.size
    b_fft = next_pow2(2n-1); g_fft=next_pow2(3n-2)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
    m=0.9; v=0; t=0; lr=0.03? beta=8
    last_score=score; last_L=L
    while time<deadline:
       c=conv(a,b_fft); r=n*c; M=max(r)
       p, L = lse_p(r,beta)
       grad_a = weighted_gradient(a,p,g_fft,n) * n? _weighted_gradient returns 2h; multiply by n.
       gmean = dot(a,grad_a)
       grad_z = a*(grad_a - gmean)
       # sanitize
       t +=1
       m=v*t*lr; v2=v+v*t*lr^2; z_new = z - m/sqrt(v2+eps)
       # clip
       z_new = nan_to_num; z_new = clip(z_new, z-5? or -80,80)
       a_new = softmax(z_new) (stable)
       # If a_new too degenerate (max >0.8) revert? 
       score_new=score(a_new)
       if finite and (score_new < best_score * (1+1e-9)):
          best update
       # accept if L_new? We can recompute L with c? Could compute r_new from c_new. But score already computed c. p_new? We can compute L with c_new and same beta: L_new=(lse). If L_new < L - 1e-12 or score_new < score: accept; else revert and maybe reduce beta? Adam state keep.
       # To faciliate exploration, if no improvement many iters, perturb.
       # beta update: every 50 iters beta*=1.05 up to beta_max (maybe 2000). If fail>500, beta*=0.5 and lr*=0.5.
       # If time left, use exact active gradient? Could compute subgradient: p= weights where r close to M.
    return best

Let's decide algorithm final in more concrete plan.

Overview:
- Use multiple strategies:
  1. Evaluation and fallback: best_start from height_sequence_1 if exists. If not, exponential.
  2. "Small-n global search" at n=64/128: many random starts with logit Adam, short time, collect good shapes.
  3. "Low-dimensional spectral parameter search" at n=256/512: sample functions generated by random low-frequency coefficients, run short optimizer, collect.
  4. Staged optimization at increasing n: 256,512,1024,2048,4096 maybe. For each length, choose top starts from previous best and low-dim shapes, run optimizer. Use a time allocation table.
  5. Final deep refine on best and best length maybe 4096/8192. 
- Use simpler/robust optimizer: custom "projection gradient with line search" instead of Adam? Need reliability.

Let's design a deterministic projected gradient (PG) that can be tuned. The objective F(a)=max c. Gradient subgradient at active lag. Smooth LSE. The previous used v=a - alpha grad_c; clip normalize. That worked to 1.523. We can improve by:
- Use exact Euclidean projection to simplex after gradient (or soft?).
- Use backtracking line search based on LSE or exact score.
- Use adaptive subgradient combining active lags softmax.
- Use restart perturbations.

Projected gradient with smooth surrogate L_β. For β large, L_β is convex? c_k not convex, but gradient computed. Still.

Line search: from a, grad = ∇L_β(a) - mean? Since constraint sum=1, use gradient projected to tangent: g_t = grad - (sum a grad?)? If using Euclidean projection to simplex, just P(a - η grad). But L is invariant under adding constant to grad? For simplex, need grad minus mean? For descent direction a - η grad; projection will adjust. Usually g = grad - mean(grad) or grad - (1/a? no). We can use g_t = grad_a - np.mean(grad_a) to preserve first-order tangent for sum? The mean of grad_a may not equal a·grad. For LSE homogeneous, gradient under sum constraint: use g_t = grad_a - (a @ grad_a) *1? Actually tangent to sum=1 is vectors d with sum d=0. Projection of gradient onto tangent Euclidean: g_proj = grad - (mean(grad))1. On simplex after projection, okay. Use mean. But natural gradient uses a@grad. Which better? For nonuniform probabilities, mean projection accounts sum; sum of a_i* (grad-mean) = a@grad - mean(grad) sum a not necessarily zero. For Euclidean sum constraint, Fermat if d = a - η proj grad and P(a) because P(a-η grad) automatically handles tangent at step? The projection onto affine hyperplane effectively uses projection of v onto hyperplane, the direction is v - mean(v). So use grad - mean(grad).

Backtracking on L_β:
    a0, L0, grad.
    eta initial.
    while true:
       v = a - eta*(grad - mean(grad))
       a_new = project_simplex(v) (Euclidean)
       L_new = lse_value(c_new, beta)
       if L_new < L0 - 1e-12*max(1,abs(L0)): accept; eta*=1.1
       else eta*=0.5; if too small restart.

Alternative: Use "dykstra" projection onto simplex after step: sort O(n log n). Maybe worth it for n<=2048. We can implement both: if n<=2048 use `_project_simplex` else soft. Let's implement Euclidean projection for accuracy.

Euclidean projection to simplex (Duchi et al):
```
def _project_simplex(v):
    v = nan_to_num(v)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, v.size+1) > (cssv - 1))[0][-1]
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1) / (rho + 1)
    w = v - theta
    w[w<0]=0
    # due to numeric, normalize to 1
    s=w.sum(); if s<=0 uniform else w/s
```
This projects to sum=1, nonnegative. O(n log n). For n=4096 sort each line search step maybe costly but acceptable if not too many. We can choose soft for line search to speed, and occasionally Euclidean? Maybe line search multiple soft projections. The previous used soft and worked. Let's keep soft for speed. Implement soft:
```
w=np.maximum(v,1e-300); s=w.sum(); return w/s
```
This is not Euclidean but smooth. Could add tiny uniform to avoid zeros.

Line search PG algorithm:
```
def _pg_optimize(a0, deadline, rng, beta_target=20, use_perturb=True):
  a=normalize; c=conv; maxc=max; score=2n maxc; best...
  eta=0.1; beta_eff=beta_target; c_scale=maxc
  state: fail=0
  while time:
    maxc=max(c)
    beta=beta_eff/maxc if maxc>0 else 1
    p, Lcur = lse_and_p(c,beta)
    grad=conv_weights(a,p)
    g = grad - grad.mean()
    # guard scale
    scale=max(abs(g))
    if scale<=0: perturb
    # backtracking
    best_accept=False
    for tries in range(12):
       v = a - eta*g
       a_new=project_soft(v)
       c_new=conv(a_new); maxc_new; score_new=2nmax; L_new=lse_value(c_new,beta)
       if L_new < Lcur - 1e-14 or score_new < best_score? Actually if accept based on L, okay.

Imports:
```
import time
import math

    import scipy.sparse as sp
    from scipy.optimize import linprog

No need scipy.signal.

Top-level helpers:
- `_safe_floats(seq)`
- `_normalize(a)`
- `_next_pow2(n)`
- `_convolve(a)` full length. Use nfft=next_pow2(2n-1). For small n maybe direct? Use FFT always.
```
def _full_conv(a):
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return empty
   if n < 64: return np.convolve(a,a) maybe faster exact
   nfft=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,nfft)[:2*n-1]
   c=np.real_if_close? c=c.real
   # small negative due roundoff; clip 0? For score max, negative not matter; for LSE, negative possible. Clip to 0 to ensure nonnegative target? The true c nonnegative; numerical -1e-18. Set c=np.maximum(c,0.0).
   return c
```
- `_score_from_conv(c,n)=2*n*max(c)`
- `_score(a)` = _score_from_conv(_full_conv(a),len(a)); if finite.
- `_lse_and_p(c,beta)`: stable. If beta <=1e-12 or c.size=0: p uniform; val mean? For LSE with beta small, LSE ≈ mean(c). If beta<=0. `m=np.max(c)`; if beta*m <=50 maybe use exp? but stable. Use p = exp(beta(c-m)); p/=sum; val = m + log(sum(exp))/beta. If beta very large and max differences, for ties p equal among max? Since c-m<=0. Use floor arg at -745. If all e zero because underflow from m? m included one exp 0 =1, so sum≥1. Good.
- `_weighted_gradient(a, p, fft_size=None)`: compute 2 * convolution p with reversed a. Need efficient. `ar=a[::-1]`. We need h_i =∑_k p_k a_{k-i} for k-i valid. We can compute full conv p * a? Let's derive. If `conv = np.convolve(p, ar)`? Previous: ar=a[::-1], conv=irfft(fft(ar)*fft(weights)), h=conv[n-1:2n-1]. Let's verify. Let r_i=a_{n-1-i}. conv(t)=∑_{j} r_j p_{t-j}. For t from n-1 to 2n-2, t-j =? We want for output i (0..n-1) sum_{k} p_k a_{k-i}. Let j=n-1-i. Then a_{k-i}=a? Set r_j = a_{n-1-j}. If k-i = n-1-j => k = n-1-i+j = n-1 + t? Maybe previous works. We'll trust. Use FFT sizes next_pow2(2*len(p)-1) maybe p length 2n-1, ar length n, conv length 3n-2. Previous g_fft=next_pow2(3*n-2). Use.
```
def _grad_from_weights(a,p):
  n=a.size
  ar=a[::-1]
  nfft=_next_pow2(len(p)+n-1)
  A=np.fft.rfft(ar,nfft); P=np.fft.rfft(p,nfft)
  conv=np.fft.irfft(A*P,nfft).real
  h=conv[n-1:2*n-1]
  h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
  return 2.0*h
```
Could optimize by computing A_r outside? a changes each iter; need rfft(ar) each step. okay.
- `_proj_soft(v, floor=1e-300)`: `w=np.maximum(v,0.0); s=w.sum(); if s<=... return uniform; return w/s`
- `_proj_euclid(v)` optional.
- `_taper(a, width)`: multiply by smooth ramp? For endpoints zero. If width<=0 return. Use x=i/(n-1), factor = sin(pi*min(width*x,1-width*x)/width)? Simpler: `f = np.ones(n); m=min(width,n//4); f[:m]=np.linspace(0,1,m+1); f[-m:]=f[:m][::-1]; return a*f normalized`. Avoid first exactly zero maybe 1e-6.
- `_resample(a, n)` as previous. Use interpolation; preserve sum. If a length small. Use endpoints. Ensure nonnegative.
- `_smooth_random(a, rng, amplitude)`: low-pass noise multiply.
- `_perturb_mix(a,best,rng)`: combine random block and best.

PG optimizer:
```
def _pg_optimize(a0, deadline, rng, max_iters=None, beta_target=16.0, use_lp=False, lp_every=0):
   a=_normalize(a0); n=a.size; if n<2 return
   c=_full_conv(a); score=...
   best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
   alpha=0.25; beta_t=beta_target; fail=0; last_best=0; it=0
   best_lp_score=best_score
   while time<deadline:
      maxc=max(c) if c.size else 0
      if maxc<=1e-300: maxc=1e-300
      beta=beta_t/maxc
      p,Lcur=_lse_and_p(c,beta)
      if fail>... change beta
      grad=_grad_from_weights(a,p)
      g=grad - np.mean(grad)
      scale=np.max(np.abs(g))
      if scale<=...:
          a=perturb; c update; continue
      # adaptive alpha reset if too small
      if alpha<0.01: alpha=0.25
      # backtracking max 10
      accepted=False
      for _ in range(12):
          v=a - alpha*g
          a_new=_proj_soft(v)
          c_new=_full_conv(a_new)
          score_new=2n max(c_new)
          # lse value with same beta
          _,? compute val_new stable maybe `_lse_value(c_new,beta)`
          if (np.isfinite(val_new) and val_new < Lcur - 1e-15) or (np.isfinite(score_new) and score_new < score - 1e-15):
             a=a_new; c=c_new; score=score_new; fail=0; alpha=min(2.0, alpha*1.1); accepted=True; break
          alpha *= 0.5
      if not accepted:
          fail +=1
          if fail % 3 ==0: beta_t *=0.9
          if fail > 200:
              a=_perturb_mix(best_a, a, rng, mix=0.4)
              c=_full_conv(a); score=...
              beta_t=max(8.0,beta_t*0.5); alpha=0.25; fail=0
      if score < best_score - 1e-14:
          best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score; last_best=it
          beta_t = min(2000.0, beta_t*1.02)
      if it % 500 ==0 and it-last_best>2000 and time<deadline-10:
          a=_perturb_mix(best_a,a,rng,0.3)
          ...
      # optional LP every some iterations: if use_lp and it%100==0 and remaining>60
      if use_lp and it % 200 ==0 and time.monotonic()<deadline-30:
          g=_lp_minimize(a)
          if g is not None:
             for mix in (0.2,0.5,1.0):
                cand=_normalize((1-mix)*a + mix*g)
                sc=_score(cand)
                if sc < best_score:
                   a=cand; c=_full_conv(a); score=sc; best...
                   break
      it +=1
   return best_a, best_score
```

def _lp_refine(a0, deadline, rng, max_solves=20):
   a=_normalize(a0 + 1e-10/(len(a0)))
   best...
   solve_count=0
   while time<deadline and solve_count<max_solves:
      if not _HAS_SCIPY or a.size>1200: break? 
      g=_lp_minimize(a, timeout_s=15)
      solve_count+=1
      candidates=[g, normalize(0.7a+0.3g), normalize(0.5a+0.5g)]
      improved=False
      for cand in candidates:
         sc=_score(cand)
         if sc<best: best=; a=cand; c=...; improved=True; break
      if not improved:
         # try random起來: g from lp of perturbed?
      if no improve 3 times: break
   return best
```

Build matrix function top-level:
```
def _lp_next_sequence(f):
   f=_normalize(f)
   f[f<1e-18]=1e-9; f=f/f.sum()
   n=f.size
   if n>1500: return None
   try:
     rows_total=2*n-1
     N=n*n
     # Memory guard: if N>2_500_000 (n>1581) return None
     col_idx = np.repeat(np.arange(n), n)
     offset = np.tile(np.arange(n), n)
     row_idx = col_idx + offset
     data = np.repeat(f, n)
     # M col
     row_m = np.arange(rows_total)
     col_m = np.full(rows_total, n)
     data_m = np.full(rows_total, -1.0)
     A = sp.csr_matrix((np.concatenate((data,data_m)), (np.concatenate((row_idx,row_m)), np.concatenate((col_idx,col_m)))), shape=(rows_total,n+1))
     c=np.zeros(n+1); c[-1]=1.0
     A_eq=np.ones((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,-1]=0.0
     b_eq=np.array([1.0])
     bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n + [(0.0,None)]
     res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.zeros(rows_total), A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
     if res.x is None: return None
     g=res.x[:n]
     g=np.asarray(g,float); g=np.nan_to_num(g); g=np.maximum(g,0)
     if g.sum()<1e-15: return None
     return g/g.sum()
   except Exception:
     return None
```
Memory for n=1024: N=1,048,576; data arrays total: row_idx/col int64 8MB each, data 8MB, concat adds another ~24MB, okay maybe 100MB. For n=1536: 2.36M -> ~60MB arrays, concat 180MB maybe okay? To be safe n<=1024. Set max_n for LP 1024.
Could optimize memory by not concatenating? HIGHS accepts list? no. Use `sp.block_diag`? no. We can append M column separately using csr_hstack? `A = sp.hstack([C, -np.eye(rows_total,1)])`? C itself requires build. Simpler okay.

def _make_starts(n,best,given,rng, max_starts):
  cands=[]
  if best is not None:
     cands.append(_resample(best,n))
     for w in [max(1,n//64), max(2,n//32), max(4,n//16), max(8,n//8)]:
        cands.append(_taper(_resample(best,n),w))
  if given and given.size>1:
     cands.append(_resample(given,n))
  # param families
  alphas=[0.4,0.8,1.5,2.5,4.0]
  for alpha in alphas:
     cands.append(_make_exp(n,alpha))
     cands.append(_make_mixture(n,alpha,0.7))
  for s,t in [(0.05,0.95),(0.1,0.9),(0.15,0.85),(0.2,0.8),(0.3,0.7),(0.5,0.5),(0.5,1.5),(1.0,2.0)]:
     cands.append(_shape_beta(n,s,t))
     # reversed?
  for α in [0.5,1,2]: for β in α*1.5,α*3:
     cands.append(_shape_diff_exp(n,α,β,0)); cands.append(...,1)
  # random lowfreq
  for k in range(4):
     cands.append(_make_smooth_random(n,rng,_resample(best,n if best),0.45))
  # clip positive and normalize
  # score and sort
```

Param functions top-level:
- `_make_uniform(n)`
- `_make_exponential(n, alpha)` with x linspace; a=exp(-alpha*x)+1e-3
- `_make_mixture(n, alpha, gamma)`: exp(-alpha*x)+gamma*exp(-alpha*(1-x)) +0.1
- `_shape_beta(n,s,t)`: x=np.linspace(0,1,n); a=x**s*(1-x)**t, endpoints handle: if s==0 then x**0=1, but 0**0 in numpy gives 1? Use `np.where(x>0, x**s, 1.0 if s==0 else 0.0)`. Simpler add eps: x_eps=np.clip(x,1e-12,1-1e-12), a=x_eps**s * (1-x_eps)**t. This gives endpoints small not zero; okay. Normalize.
- `_shape_diff_exp(n, alpha, beta, right=False)`: x as above; y=x if not right else 1-x; if beta<alpha swap; a=np.exp(-alpha*y)-np.exp(-beta*y); a=np.maximum(a,1e-12); normalize.
- `_make_smooth_random(n,rng,base,amp)`: as previous.

Pseudo full:
```
def _pg_optimize(a0, deadline, rng=None, beta_target=16.0, use_array_prefill=False):
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(1)
    a=_normalize(a0)
    n=a.size
    if n<3: return a, _score(a)
    c=_full_conv(a); score=_score_from_conv(c,n); best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
    alpha=0.25; beta_t=beta_target; fail=0; it=0; last_best=0
    best_exact=score
    # keep a copy of start for occasional
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        maxc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if maxc < 1e-300: maxc=1e-300
        beta=beta_t / maxc
        p, Lcur = _lse_and_p(c,beta)
        grad=_grad_from_weights(a,p)
        g=grad - float(np.mean(grad))
        scale=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if scale < 1e-300:
            a=_perturb_mix(best_a,a,rng,0.35)
            c=_full_conv(a); score=...; continue
        if alpha < 0.02:
            alpha=0.25; fail=0
        alpha_base=alpha
        accepted=False
        for _ in range(12):
            v = a - alpha*g
            a_new = _proj_soft(v)
            c_new = _full_conv(a_new)
            score_new = 2.0*n*max(c_new)
            Lnew = _lse_value(c_new, beta)
            if np.isfinite(score_new) and (np.isfinite(Lnew) and Lnew < Lcur - 1e-15 or score_new < score - 1e-15):
                a=a_new; c=c_new; score=score_new; fail=0; accepted=True; alpha=min(2.0,alpha*1.08); break
            alpha *= 0.45
        if not accepted:
            alpha=alpha_base
            fail +=1
            if fail % 2 == 0:
                beta_t = max(6.0, beta_t*0.9)
            if fail > 80:
                # local perturbation from best
                a = _perturb_mix(best_a, a, rng, min(0.6, 0.2+0.01*fail))
                c = _full_conv(a); score=...
                beta_t = max(8.0, beta_t*0.5)
                alpha=0.25
                fail=0
                continue
        if score < best_score - 1e-14:
            best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score; last_best=it
            beta_t=min(3000.0, beta_t*1.03)
        # periodic sharpen / restart
        if it % 200 == 0:
            beta_t=min(3000.0, beta_t*1.1)
        if it - last_best > 1200 and fail<20 and time.monotonic()<deadline-5:
            # try long perturbation to escape
            a=_perturb_mix(best_a,a,rng,0.45)
            c=_full_conv(a); score=...
            beta_t=12.0; alpha=0.25; fail=0
        it +=1
    return best_a,best_score
```
But line search uses Lcur with current c. If beta is huge, Lcur close maxc. Lnew may not decrease due numerical when exact max same. Accept if score_new < score? We included OR score_new < score. Good.
Potential issue: Since `v = a - alpha*g`, g mean-subtracted, sum v =1. Soft projection may alter sum if negative clipped; if many negative, sum less, normalization increases positives. Fine.

Maybe use gradient `g=grad - np.mean(grad)`; but for simplex constraint, the correct projection of gradient onto hyperplane is subtract mean. Good.

LSE value function:
```
def _lse_value(c,beta):
 if c.size==0: return 0
 m=np.max(c)
 if beta<=1e-12: return float(np.mean(c))
 arg=beta*(c-m); arg[arg<-745]=-745; e=np.exp(arg); s=e.sum(); return m + np.log(s)/beta
```
For p similarly. If beta very large, p one-hot on max; gradient okay.

Potential speed: `_lse_value(c_new,beta)` computes exp and log each line search; many. But c length 2n; okay. We can avoid if beta large and check score only. But keep.

Could use `maxc_new` for exact; Lnew uses same beta. If beta large, exp on c-m; c-m at most maxc; beta*maxc ~β_eff (e.g., 16) not too large because beta=β_eff/maxc, so beta*(c-m)≤β_eff ≤3000, safe. Good! We choose beta_t as effective β (16-3000). So LSE safe and scale manageable. Nice.

After fail, beta_t lowers. After improve, beta_t increases. Good.

`_perturb_mix` implementation:
```
def _perturb_mix(best, a, rng, strength=0.35):
 n=best.size
 m=strength? 
 # Start from blend between a and best
 base = np.maximum(0, (1-strength)*a + strength*best)
 # choose 1-3 blocks
 out=base.copy()
 nblocks=rng.integers(1,3)
 for _ in range(nblocks):
    w=int(max(2, n//rng.integers(8,32)))
    s=int(rng.integers(0,n-w))
    fac=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.4, size=w)))
    out[s:s+w] *= fac
 # plus small noise
 out = out + 1e-6/(n) * rng.uniform(size=n)
 return _normalize(out)
```
Ensure no closure. Good.

Maybe if perturbation zeros out many, fallback.

```
def _lp_refine(a0, deadline, rng, max_solves=25, max_n=1024):
  a=_normalize(a0); n=a.size
  if not _HAS_SCIPY or n>max_n: return a, _score(a)
  c=_full_conv(a); best_score=...; best_a=a.copy()
  no_improve=0
  solves=0
  while time.monotonic() < deadline and solves<max_solves:
     f = _normalize(a + 1e-10 / n)  # positive floor
     t_start=time.monotonic()
     g=_lp_next_sequence(f)
     solves+=1
     if g is None or time.monotonic()>deadline: break
     tested=False
     # Ensure g smooth? blend candidates
     if np.max(np.abs(np.diff(g))) > 10*np.mean? no.
     for mix in (0.2,0.4,0.6,1.0):
        cand = _normalize((1-mix)*a + mix*g)
        sc=_score(cand)
        if sc < best_score:
           best_a=cand; best_score=sc; a=cand; c=_full_conv(a); no_improve=0; tested=True; break
        if sc < _score(a): # maybe accept cross improvement not best? Could update a to move
           a=cand; c=_full_conv(a); tested=True
     if not tested:
        no_improve +=1
     if no_improve >=3:
        break
     # if LP solve very slow >30s and no improve break? if time_left<30 break
     if time.monotonic() - t_start > 45 and no_improve>0: break
  return best_a,best_score
```

import time

Top-level functions:
```
def _normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    n=a.size
    if n==0 or s<=1e-300:
        n=max(n,1)
        return np.ones(n)/float(n)
    return a/s
```

`_next_pow2`.

`_full_conv`:
```
def _full_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    if n < 96:
       c=np.convolve(a,a)
       c=np.asarray(c,float)
       return np.maximum(c,0.0)
    nfft=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,nfft)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    # small roundoff negatives
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
Could sum c not 1 due roundoff clip; peak may under/over but okay. For exact best, maybe using direct np.convolve final? Evaluator uses direct. Our internal score for n<96 direct. For n>=96 FFT. At final, evaluator direct can differ tiny. No issue.

`_score_from_conv(c,n)`:
```
if c.size==0: return 1e100
m=float(np.max(c))
if not np.isfinite(m): return 1e100
return 2.0*float(n)*m
```

`_score(a)` = score from full conv.

`_lse_and_p`:
```
def _lse_and_p(c,beta):
   c=np.asarray(c,float)
   if c.size==0: return np.ones(1),0.0
   m=float(np.max(c))
   if beta <= 1e-12:
       p=np.full(c.size,1.0/c.size)
       val=float(np.mean(c))
       return p,val
   arg=beta*(c-m)
   arg=np.maximum(arg,-745.0)
   e=np.exp(arg)
   s=float(e.sum())
   if s<=0:
      p=np.one; val=m
   else:
      p=e/s
      val=m + float(np.log(s))/beta
   return p,val
```
`_lse_value` analogous.

`_grad_from_weights`:
```
def _grad_from_weights(a,p):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   p=np.asarray(p,float)
   n=a.size
   ar=a[::-1]
   nfft=_next_pow2(n+p.size-1)
   A=np.fft.rfft(ar,nfft)
   P=np.fft.rfft(p,nfft)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(A*P,nfft).real
   if conv.size < 2*n-1:
      # pad?
      conv=np.pad(conv,(0,2*n-1-conv.size))?
   h=conv[n-1:2*n-1]
   h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
   return 2.0*h
```
Since nfft≥len sum, conv length at least. Good.

`_proj_soft`:
```
def _proj_soft(v):
   v=np.nan_to_num(v)
   w=np.maximum(v,0.0)
   s=float(w.sum())
   if s<=1e-300:
       u=np.ones_like(v)
       return u/u.size
   return w/s
```

Euclidean:
```
def _proj_euclid(v):
    v=np.nan_to_num(v).ravel().astype(float)
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return np.ones(1)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1,n+1) > (cssv - 1.0))[0]
    if rho.size==0:
        rho=np.array([0])
    else:
        rho=rho[-1]
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1.0)/float(rho+1)
    w=v-theta
    w=np.maximum(w,0.0)
    s=float(w.sum())
    if s<=1e-300: return np.ones(n)/n
    return w/s
```
Maybe use when n<300.

`_resample_sequence`:
```
def _resample_sequence(old,new_n):
    old=_normalize(old)
    if old.size==1: return np.full(new_n,1.0/new_n)
    if old.size==new_n: return old
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,old.size)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
    new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,old.values?)
    return _normalize(new)
```
Use `np.interp`.

`_taper`:
```
def _taper(a,width):
    a=_normalize(a)
    n=a.size
    m=int(min(max(0,int(width)), n//3))
    if m<=1: return a
    f=np.ones(n)
    ramp=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m+1)
    ramp[0]=1e-6
    f[:m]=ramp[:-1]
    f[-m:]=ramp[:-1][::-1]
    c=a*f; c=np.maximum(c,1e-12)
    return _normalize(c)
```
If m=1, no.

`_make_uniform`, `_make_exponential`, `_make_mixture`, `_shape_beta`, `_shape_diff_exp`, `_make_smooth_random`, `_perturb_mix`.

`_make_smooth_random`:
```
def _make_smooth_random(n,rng,base=None,amplitude=0.4):
   n=max(int(n),2)
   if base is None: base=_make_uniform(n)
   else: base=_normalize(base)
   z=rng.normal(size=n)
   F=np.fft.rfft(z)
   cutoff=max(2, min(len(F), n//16+2))
   if cutoff < len(F): F[cutoff:]*=0
   f=np.fft.irfft(F,n)
   f=f-f.mean()
   sd=float(np.std(f))
   if sd>1e-12: f=f/sd
   a=base*(1+amplitude*f)
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-12)
   return _normalize(a)
```
If cutoff==len, no.

`_perturb_mix`:
```
def _perturb_mix(best,a,rng,strength=0.35):
    best=_normalize(best); a=_normalize(a); n=best.size
    if strength>1: strength=1
    base = (1-strength)*a + strength*best
    base = np.maximum(base,1e-12)
    nb=int(rng.integers(1, min(4, max(2, n//64+1))))
    for _ in range(nb):
       w=int(max(2, min(n//3, n//int(rng.integers(8,32)))))
       s=int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-w+1)))
       fac=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, size=w))
       base[s:s+w] *= fac
    base = base + (1e-6/n)*rng.uniform(size=n)
    return _normalize(base)
```
Need ensure `n//int` if n small. Use `den=int(rng.integers(8,33)); w=max(2,min(n//3,n//den))`. For n<16, n//den maybe 0; max2.

Now, `_lp_next_sequence`: Top-level. Use np.repeat/tile. Need wrap in try. If n=1? return uniform.

```
def _lp_next_sequence(f, timeout_s=25.0):
   if not _HAS_SCIPY: return None
   f=_normalize(f)
   n=f.size
   if n < 2: return f.copy()
   if n > 1024: return None
   f = f + 1e-9/float(n); f=f/f.sum()
   try:
      rows=2*n-1
      N=n*n
      if N > 1_200_000: return None # maybe n>1095
      cols=np.repeat(np.arange(n), n)
      off=np.tile(np.arange(n), n)
      rows_idx=cols+off
      data=np.repeat(f, n)
      row_m=np.arange(rows)
      col_m=np.full(rows,n,dtype=np.int64)
      data_m=np.full(rows,-1.0,np.float64)
      all_data=np.concatenate((data,data_m))
      all_rows=np.concatenate((rows_idx,row_m))
      all_cols=np.concatenate((cols,col_m))
      A=_sp.csr_matrix((all_data,(all_rows,all_cols)), shape=(rows,n+1))
      cobj=np.zeros(n+1); cobj[-1]=1.0
      Aeq=np.ones((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,-1]=0.0
      beq=np.array([1.0])
      bounds=[(0.0,None) for _ in range(n+1)]
      opts={'time_limit': float(max(5.0,timeout_s)), 'presolve': True}
      try:
         res=_linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.zeros(rows), A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=beq, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options=opts)
      except Exception:
         try:
            res=_linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.zeros(rows), A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=beq, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
         except Exception:
            res=None
      if res is None or getattr(res,'x',None) is None: return None
      status=int(getattr(res,'status',-1))
      if status not in (0,3): return None
      g=np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
      g=np.nan_to_num(g); g=np.maximum(g,0.0); s=g.sum()
      if s<=1e-15: return None
      return g/s
   except Exception:
      return None
```
Memory: N maybe 1,048,576 for n1024. `np.concatenate` doubles. okay. If MemoryError caught return None.

def _lp_refine(a0, deadline, rng, max_solves=20):
   a=_normalize(a0); n=a.size
   c=_full_conv(a); score=_score_from_conv(c,n); best...
   if not _HAS_SCIPY or n>1024: return a,score
   no=0; solves=0
   while time.monotonic()<deadline and solves<max_solves:
      remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
      if remaining<8: break
      timeout_s=min(25.0, max(5.0, remaining-3.0))
      g=_lp_next_sequence(a, timeout_s)
      solves+=1
      if g is None: break
      improved=False
      best_candidate=None; best_cand_score=_score(a)
      for mix in (0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,1.0):
         cand=_normalize((1.0-mix)*a + mix*g)
         sc=_score(cand)
         if sc < best_cand_score:
            best_cand_score=sc; best_candidate=cand
      if best_candidate is not None:
         a=best_candidate; c=_full_conv(a); score=best_cand_score; no=0
         if score < best_score: best...
         improved=True
      else:
         no+=1
      if not improved and time.monotonic() - startsolve > 40: break
      if no>=3: break
   return best_a,best_score
```

Now, `_build_starts` with scoring may be expensive if max_starts small but candidates many. We'll limit candidates to ~48. Implement:
```
def _build_starts(n,best_a,given,rng,max_starts):
   cands=[]; seen=[]
   nb=max(2,int(n))
   if best_a is not None:
      bb=_resample_sequence(best_a,n)
      _add_candidate(cands,seen,bb)
      for w in (2,4,8,16,32, max(1,n//32), max(2,n//16)): _add_candidate(cands,seen,_taper(bb,w))
   if given is not None: _add_candidate(cands,seen,_resample_sequence(given,n))
   for s in _param_shapes(n,rng): _add_candidate(cands,seen,s)
   # random smooth from best/uniform
   base = _resample_sequence(best_a,n) if best_a is not None else _make_uniform(n)
   for _ in range(3): _add_candidate(cands,seen,_make_smooth_random(n,rng,base,0.45))
   if len(cands) > 80:
      idx = rng.choice(len(cands), size=80, replace=False)
      cands = [cands[int(i)] for i in idx]
   scored=[]
   for i,a in enumerate(cands):
      sc=_score(a)
      if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc,i))
   scored.sort()
   out=[]
   for _,i in scored[:max_starts]:
      out.append(cands[i])
   if not out:
      out=[cands[0] if cands else _make_uniform(n)]
   return out
```
`rng.choice` returns numpy array; if len small choice size min. Use `size=min(80,len(cands))`.

def _add_candidate(cands, seen, a):

    step=max(1,a.size//32)
    sig=tuple(np.round(a[::step], 6).tolist())
    if sig not in seen:
        seen.append(sig); cands.append(a)
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative sequences minimizing
    score(a) = 2 n max_k (a*a)_k / (sum a)^2.

The method combines:
  * many smoothly generated starts, including asymmetric beta/exponential
    pieces and low-frequency random perturbations;
  * a projected subgradient method on a sharpened log-sum-exp surrogate of
    max(a*a), using FFT-based gradients;
  * an auxiliary linear-programming operator that, for a fixed current
    direction f, finds a nonnegative sequence g of sum one minimizing
    max(f*g); blends of f and g are accepted only if they lower the exact
    score;
  * staged length upsampling and final local post-processing (smoothing,
    tapering, exponential reweighting).
"""

import time
import numpy as np

np.seterr(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore", divide="ignore")

try:
    import scipy.sparse as _sp
    from scipy.optimize import linprog as _linprog
    _HAS_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _sp = None
    _linprog = None
    _HAS_SCIPY = False


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or s <= 1e-300:
        n = max(n, 1)
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _full_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n < 96:
        c = np.asarray(np.convolve(a, a), dtype=np.float64)
        return np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    nfft = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, nfft)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def _score_from_conv(c, n):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 1e100
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return 1e100
    return 2.0 * float(n) * m


def _score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    c = _full_conv(a)
    return _score_from_conv(c, a.size)


def _lse_value(c, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if beta <= 1e-12:
        return float(np.mean(c))
    arg = beta * (c - m)
    arg = np.maximum(arg, -745.0)
    e = np.exp(arg)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return m
    return m + float(np.log(s)) / beta


def _lse_and_p(c, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if beta <= 1e-12:
        p = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
        return p, float(np.mean(c))
    arg = beta * (c - m)
    arg = np.maximum(arg, -745.0)
    e = np.exp(arg)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        p = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
        return p, m
    p = e / s
    val = m + float(np.log(s)) / beta
    return p, val


def _grad_from_weights(a, p):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    ar = a[::-1]
    nfft = _next_pow2(n + p.size - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(ar, nfft)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, nfft)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * P, nfft).real
    conv = np.asarray(conv, dtype=np.float64)
    if conv.size < 2 * n - 1:
        conv = np.concatenate([conv, np.zeros(2 * n - 1 - conv.size, dtype=np.float64)])
    h = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    return 2.0 * h


def _proj_soft(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    w = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        u = np.ones_like(v, dtype=np.float64)
        return u / float(u.size)
    return w / s


def _proj_euclid(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    good = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, n + 1) > (cssv - 1.0))[0]
    if good.size == 0:
        rho = np.int64(0)
    else:
        rho = np.int64(good[-1])
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / float(rho + 1)
    w = v - theta
    w = np.maximum(w, 0.0)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return w / s


def _resample_sequence(old, new_n):
    old = _normalize(old)
    new_n = max(2, int(new_n))
    if old.size == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    if old.size == 1:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    if old.size == new_n:
        return old.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, old)
    return _normalize(new)


def _taper(a, width):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    m = int(min(max(0, int(width)), max(1, n // 3)))
    if m <= 1:
        return a
    f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    ramp = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    ramp[0] = 1e-6
    f[:m] = ramp[:-1]
    f[-m:] = ramp[:-1][::-1]
    c = np.maximum(a * f, 1e-12)
    return _normalize(c)


def _box_smooth(a, m):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    m = int(max(1, min(m, n // 3)))
    k = np.ones(2 * m + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    k /= float(k.sum())
    c = np.convolve(a, k, mode="same")
    c = np.maximum(c, 1e-12)
    return _normalize(c)


def _reweight_exp(a, gamma, right=False):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    y = (1.0 - x) if right else x
    w = np.exp(float(gamma) * y)
    return _normalize(a * w)


def _make_uniform(n):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)


def _make_exponential(n, alpha):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.exp(-float(alpha) * x) + 1e-8
    return _normalize(a)


def _make_mixture(n, alpha, gamma):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.exp(-float(alpha) * x) + float(gamma) * np.exp(-float(alpha) * (1.0 - x))
    a = a + 0.12
    return _normalize(a)


def _shape_beta(n, s, t):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.clip(x, 1e-12, 1.0 - 1e-12)
    a = np.power(x, float(s)) * np.power(1.0 - x, float(t))
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    return _normalize(a)


def _shape_diff_exp(n, alpha, beta, right=False):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    y = (1.0 - x) if right else x
    if beta < alpha:
        alpha, beta = beta, alpha
    a = np.exp(-float(alpha) * y) - np.exp(-float(beta) * y)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-9)
    return _normalize(a)


def _make_smooth_random(n, rng, base=None, amplitude=0.45):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    if base is None:
        base = _make_uniform(n)
    base = _normalize(base)
    z = rng.normal(size=n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(z)
    cutoff = max(2, min(len(F), n // 16 + 2))
    if cutoff < len(F):
        F[cutoff:] = 0.0
    f = np.fft.irfft(F, n)
    f = f - float(np.mean(f))
    sd = float(np.std(f))
    if sd > 1e-12:
        f = f / sd
    a = base * (1.0 + float(amplitude) * f)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    return _normalize(a)


def _perturb_mix(best, a, rng, strength=0.35):
    best = _normalize(best)
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = best.size
    strength = float(min(1.0, max(0.0, strength)))
    base = (1.0 - strength) * a + strength * best
    base = np.maximum(base, 1e-12)
    nb = int(rng.integers(1, min(4, max(2, n // 64 + 1))))
    for _ in range(nb):
        den = int(rng.integers(8, 33))
        w = max(2, min(n // 3, n // max(1, den)))
        if w >= n:
            w = max(2, n // 4)
        s = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
        fac = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, size=w))
        base[s:s + w] *= fac
    base = base + (1e-6 / float(n)) * rng.uniform(size=n)
    return _normalize(base)


def _add_candidate(cands, seen, a):
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    step = max(1, a.size // 32)
    sig = tuple(np.round(a[::step], 6).tolist())
    if sig not in seen:
        seen.append(sig)
        cands.append(a)


def _param_shapes(n, rng):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    out = []
    out.append(_make_exponential(n, 0.75))
    out.append(_make_exponential(n, 1.5))
    out.append(_make_mixture(n, 1.0, 0.65))
    out.append(_make_mixture(n, 2.2, 0.35))
    for s, t in ((0.05, 0.95), (0.10, 0.90), (0.18, 0.82), (0.30, 0.70),
                 (0.50, 1.50), (0.90, 2.50), (0.05, 2.00)):
        out.append(_shape_beta(n, s, t))
    for al, be in ((0.45, 1.35), (1.00, 3.00), (1.60, 5.00), (0.75, 2.50)):
        out.append(_shape_diff_exp(n, al, be, False))
        out.append(_shape_diff_exp(n, al, be, True))
    base = _make_uniform(n)
    for _ in range(3):
        out.append(_make_smooth_random(n, rng, base, 0.7))
    return out


def _build_starts(n, best_a, given, rng, max_starts):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    max_starts = max(1, int(max_starts))
    cands = []
    seen = []

    if best_a is not None:
        bb = _resample_sequence(best_a, n)
        _add_candidate(cands, seen, bb)
        for w in (2, 4, 8, 16, 32, max(1, n // 32), max(2, n // 16)):
            _add_candidate(cands, seen, _taper(bb, w))

    if given is not None:
        _add_candidate(cands, seen, _resample_sequence(given, n))

    for s in _param_shapes(n, rng):
        _add_candidate(cands, seen, s)

    base = _make_uniform(n)
    if best_a is not None:
        base = 0.6 * _resample_sequence(best_a, n) + 0.4 * _make_uniform(n)
    base = _normalize(base)
    for _ in range(3):
        _add_candidate(cands, seen, _make_smooth_random(n, rng, base, 0.45))

    if len(cands) > 80:
        size = min(80, len(cands))
        idx = rng.choice(len(cands), size=size, replace=False)
        cands = [cands[int(i)] for i in idx]

    if not cands:
        return [_make_uniform(n)]

    scored = []
    for i, a in enumerate(cands):
        sc = _score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((float(sc), i))
    scored.sort()

    out = []
    for _, i in scored[:max_starts]:
        out.append(cands[int(i)])
    if not out:
        out.append(cands[0])
    return out


def _pg_optimize(a0, deadline, rng, beta_target=10.0):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return a, _score(a)

    c = _full_conv(a)
    score = _score_from_conv(c, n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score

    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)

    alpha = 0.22
    beta_t = float(beta_target)
    fail = 0
    it = 0
    last_best = 0
    use_euclid = n <= 256

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        maxc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if maxc <= 1e-300:
            maxc = 1e-300
        beta = beta_t / maxc

        p, Lcur = _lse_and_p(c, beta)
        grad = _grad_from_weights(a, p)
        g = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
        base_scale = float(np.mean(np.abs(grad)))
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if scale <= 1e-10 * max(1e-300, base_scale):
            p_u = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size) if c.size else 1.0)
            grad = _grad_from_weights(a, p_u)
            g = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
            scale = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if scale <= 1e-300:
            a = _perturb_mix(best_a, a, rng, 0.35)
            c = _full_conv(a)
            score = _score_from_conv(c, n)
            it += 1
            continue

        if alpha < 0.02:
            alpha = 0.22
            fail = 0

        step = alpha
        start_alpha = alpha
        accepted = False

        for _ in range(15):
            v = a - step * g
            a_new = _proj_euclid(v) if use_euclid else _proj_soft(v)
            c_new = _full_conv(a_new)
            score_new = _score_from_conv(c_new, n)
            Lnew = _lse_value(c_new, beta)
            if np.isfinite(score_new) and ((np.isfinite(Lnew) and Lnew < Lcur - 1e-15)
                                           or score_new < score - 1e-15):
                a = a_new
                c = c_new
                score = score_new
                fail = 0
                alpha = min(2.0, float(step) * 1.1)
                accepted = True
                break
            step *= 0.45
            if step <= 0.0015:
                break

        if not accepted:
            alpha = max(0.02, float(start_alpha) * 0.5)
            fail += 1
            if fail % 3 == 0:
                beta_t = max(5.0, beta_t * 0.9)
            if fail > 90:
                a = _perturb_mix(best_a, a, rng, 0.35 + 0.004 * fail)
                c = _full_conv(a)
                score = _score_from_conv(c, n)
                beta_t = max(8.0, beta_t * 0.55)
                alpha = 0.22
                fail = 0
                it += 1
                continue

        if score < best_score - 1e-14:
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_score = score
            last_best = it
            beta_t = min(2500.0, beta_t * 1.04)

        if it % 200 == 0:
            beta_t = min(2500.0, beta_t * 1.08)

        if it - last_best > 1500 and fail < 30 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
            a = _perturb_mix(best_a, a, rng, 0.45)
            c = _full_conv(a)
            score = _score_from_conv(c, n)
            beta_t = 10.0
            alpha = 0.22
            fail = 0

        it += 1

    return best_a, best_score


def _lp_next_sequence(f, timeout_s=25.0):
    if not _HAS_SCIPY:
        return None
    f = _normalize(f)
    n = f.size
    if n < 2:
        return f.copy()
    if n > 1024:
        return None
    f = f + (1e-9 / float(n))
    f = f / float(f.sum())

    try:
        rows = 2 * n - 1
        N = n * n
        if N > 1_200_000:
            return None
        cols = np.repeat(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64), n)
        off = np.tile(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64), n)
        row_idx = cols + off
        data = np.repeat(f, n)

        row_m = np.arange(rows, dtype=np.int64)
        col_m = np.full(rows, n, dtype=np.int64)
        data_m = np.full(rows, -1.0, dtype=np.float64)

        all_data = np.concatenate((data, data_m))
        all_rows = np.concatenate((row_idx, row_m))
        all_cols = np.concatenate((cols, col_m))

        A = _sp.csr_matrix((all_data, (all_rows, all_cols)), shape=(rows, n + 1))
        b_ub = np.zeros(rows, dtype=np.float64)

        cobj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        cobj[-1] = 1.0

        Aeq = np.ones((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        Aeq[0, -1] = 0.0
        beq = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, None) for _ in range(n + 1)]

        opts = {"time_limit": float(max(5.0, timeout_s)), "presolve": True}
        try:
            res = _linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=beq,
                           bounds=bounds, method="highs", options=opts)
        except Exception:
            try:
                res = _linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=beq,
                               bounds=bounds, method="highs",
                               options={"presolve": True})
            except Exception:
                res = None

        if res is None or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
            return None
        status = int(getattr(res, "status", -1))
        if status not in (0, 3):
            return None
        g = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        s = float(g.sum())
        if s <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(g).all():
            return None
        return g / s
    except Exception:
        return None


def _lp_refine(a0, deadline, rng, max_solves=6):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = a.size
    c = _full_conv(a)
    score = _score_from_conv(c, n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score

    if not _HAS_SCIPY or n > 1024:
        return best_a, best_score

    no_improve = 0
    solves = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and solves < int(max_solves):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 8.0:
            break
        t_solve_start = time.monotonic()
        g = _lp_next_sequence(a, timeout_s=min(30.0, max(5.0, rem - 3.0)))
        solves += 1
        if g is None:
            break

        cur_score = score
        best_cand = None
        best_cand_score = cur_score
        for mix in (0.15, 0.30, 0.50, 0.70, 1.00):
            cand = _normalize((1.0 - mix) * a + mix * g)
            sc = _score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_cand_score:
                best_cand_score = sc
                best_cand = cand

        if best_cand is not None:
            a = best_cand
            c = _full_conv(a)
            score = best_cand_score
            no_improve = 0
            if score < best_score:
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_score = score
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        wall = time.monotonic() - t_solve_start
        if no_improve >= 4:
            break
        if wall > 45.0 and no_improve > 0:
            break

    return best_a, best_score


def _update_best(best_a, best_score, a, s):
    if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
        return np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy(), float(s)
    return best_a, best_score


def _postprocess(a, deadline):
    a = _normalize(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _score(a)

    candidates = [best_a]
    for m in (1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 12):
        candidates.append(_box_smooth(a, m))
    for w in (1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256):
        candidates.append(_taper(a, w))
    for g in (0.35, 0.75, 1.5, 3.0):
        candidates.append(_reweight_exp(a, g, False))
        candidates.append(_reweight_exp(a, g, True))
    for m in (1, 3, 6):
        for w in (2, 8, 32):
            candidates.append(_taper(_box_smooth(a, m), w))

    for cand in candidates:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        cand = _normalize(cand)
        sc = _score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-14:
            best_a = cand.copy()
            best_score = sc

    return best_a, best_score


def _get_global_sequence():
    seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if seq is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    if arr.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(arr).all():
        return None
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return arr / s


def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 2400.0

    deadline = t0 + max(30.0, budget) - min(30.0, max(5.0, 0.02 * budget))
    if deadline <= t0 + 15.0:
        deadline = t0 + max(10.0, budget - 2.0)
    deadline = min(deadline, t0 + max(10.0, budget - 1.0))

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    given = _get_global_sequence()

    if given is not None:
        if given.size > 8192:
            best_a = _resample_sequence(given, 4096)
        else:
            best_a = given.copy()
    else:
        best_a = _make_exponential(256, 1.0)
    best_score = _score(best_a)
    print(f"initial len={best_a.size} score={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

    scale = min(1.0, max(0.25, budget / 2400.0))
    stages = [
        (128, 4, 10, True),
        (256, 6, 30, True),
        (512, 5, 50, True),
        (1024, 4, 80, True),
        (2048, 3, 110, False),
        (4096, 2, 180, False),
    ]

    for n, num, sec, use_lp in stages:
        sec = max(5, int(sec * scale))
        num = max(1, int(num * scale))
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - sec - 12.0:
            break

        n = int(n)
        starts = _build_starts(n, best_a, given, rng, max_starts=num)
        for idx, a0 in enumerate(starts):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 10.0:
                break
            sec_use = min(sec, max(5.0, rem))
            t_end = time.monotonic() + sec_use

            lp_time = 0.0
            if use_lp and n <= 512:
                lp_time = min(22.0, 0.35 * sec_use)
            pg_end = max(time.monotonic() + 3.0, t_end - lp_time)
            pg_end = min(pg_end, t_end)

            a, s = _pg_optimize(a0, pg_end, rng)
            best_a, best_score = _update_best(best_a, best_score, a, s)
            print(f"stage n={n} start={idx + 1}/{len(starts)} score={s:.10f} best={best_score:.10f} "
                  f"t={time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s", flush=True)

            if use_lp and n <= 512 and time.monotonic() < t_end - 6.0 and s < best_score + 0.08:
                a, s = _lp_refine(a, t_end, rng, max_solves=5)
                best_a, best_score = _update_best(best_a, best_score, a, s)
                print(f"lp n={n} score={s:.10f} best={best_score:.10f} "
                      f"t={time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s", flush=True)

    for target in (1024, 2048, 4096):
        if best_a.size >= target:
            continue
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 55.0:
            break
        d = min(120.0, max(30.0, 0.22 * rem))
        init = _resample_sequence(best_a, target)
        init = _normalize(0.88 * init + 0.12 * _make_exponential(target, 1.0))
        a, s = _pg_optimize(init, time.monotonic() + d, rng)
        best_a, best_score = _update_best(best_a, best_score, a, s)
        print(f"upscale to {a.size} score={s:.10f} best={best_score:.10f} "
              f"t={time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s", flush=True)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 12.0:
        a, s = _pg_optimize(best_a, time.monotonic() + max(8.0, rem - 2.0), rng)
        best_a, best_score = _update_best(best_a, best_score, a, s)
        print(f"final polish score={s:.10f} best={best_score:.10f} "
              f"t={time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s", flush=True)

    best_a, best_score = _postprocess(best_a, deadline)
    print(f"postprocess best={best_score:.10f} len={best_a.size}", flush=True)

    if best_a.size > 8192:
        best_a = _resample_sequence(best_a, 4096)
    best_a = _normalize(best_a)
    out = [float(x) for x in best_a]

    ev_fn = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
    if callable(ev_fn) and best_a.size <= 1024 and time.monotonic() < deadline + 5.0:
        try:
            ev = float(ev_fn(out))
            print(f"actual evaluate_sequence={ev:.10f}", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return out
```
